import sqlite3
import pandas as pd
import os

DB_PATH = "candidates.db"

def init_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    # Table to store raw discovered wallets
    cursor.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS discovered_whales (
        wallet_address TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        discovery_source TEXT,
        discovered_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        is_sniper BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0
    )
    ''')
    
    # Table to store all historical scores
    cursor.execute('''
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS historical_scores (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        wallet_address TEXT,
        win_rate REAL,
        net_profit REAL,
        avg_hold_hours REAL,
        lane TEXT,
        scored_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        FOREIGN KEY(wallet_address) REFERENCES discovered_whales(wallet_address)
    )
    ''')
    
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    print(f"[+] Initialized {DB_PATH}")

def add_discovered_whales(wallets_df, source, is_sniper=False):
    if wallets_df.empty: return 0
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    added_count = 0
    for _, row in wallets_df.iterrows():
        wallet = row.get('wallet_address') or row.get('whale_wallet')
        if not wallet: continue
        
        try:
            cursor.execute('''
            INSERT INTO discovered_whales (wallet_address, discovery_source, is_sniper)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ''', (wallet, source, is_sniper))
            added_count += 1
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
            # Wallet already exists, skip
            pass
            
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    return added_count

def get_wallets_to_score(limit=50):
    # Get wallets that have NEVER been scored, OR haven't been scored in 24 hours.
    # Prioritize unscored wallets first.
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    
    query = """
    SELECT dw.wallet_address, dw.is_sniper, MAX(hs.scored_at) as last_scored
    FROM discovered_whales dw
    LEFT JOIN historical_scores hs ON dw.wallet_address = hs.wallet_address
    GROUP BY dw.wallet_address
    HAVING last_scored IS NULL OR last_scored < datetime('now', '-24 hours')
    ORDER BY last_scored ASC NULLS FIRST
    LIMIT ?
    """
    
    df = pd.read_sql_query(query, conn, params=(limit,))
    conn.close()
    return df

def insert_score(wallet, win_rate, net_profit, avg_hold_hours, lane):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute('''
    INSERT INTO historical_scores (wallet_address, win_rate, net_profit, avg_hold_hours, lane)
    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    ''', (wallet, win_rate, net_profit, avg_hold_hours, lane))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def generate_approved_watchlist(out_path="c:/FruxLabs/Alpha-Whales/approved_watchlist.csv"):
    if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):
        return 0
        
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    
    # Get the MOST RECENT score for each wallet
    query = """
    SELECT wallet_address as whale_wallet, win_rate, net_profit, lane,
           ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY wallet_address ORDER BY scored_at DESC) as rn
    FROM historical_scores
    """
    try:
        df = pd.read_sql_query(query, conn)
    except:
        conn.close()
        return 0
    conn.close()
    
    if df.empty: return []
    
    # Keep only the latest row for each wallet
    latest_df = df[df['rn'] == 1].copy()
    
    approved = []
    
    for _, row in latest_df.iterrows():
        wallet = row['whale_wallet']
        lane = row['lane']
        wr = row['win_rate']
        profit = row['net_profit']
        
        if lane == 'Rejected':
            continue
            
        if lane == 'Sniper':
            if wr >= 0.40 and profit >= 10.0:
                approved.append(row)
        else:
            if wr >= 0.40 and profit >= 25.0:
                approved.append(row)
                
    if not approved:
        return []
        
    final_df = pd.DataFrame(approved)
    final_df['total_trades'] = 100 
    final_df['profit_factor'] = 1.0 
    
    final_df = final_df[['whale_wallet', 'total_trades', 'win_rate', 'profit_factor', 'net_profit', 'lane']]
    final_df = final_df.sort_values(by='net_profit', ascending=False)
    
    final_df.to_csv(out_path, index=False)
    return final_df['whale_wallet'].tolist()

import os
import asyncio
from telethon import TelegramClient
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()
API_ID = int(os.getenv('TG_API_ID'))
API_HASH = os.getenv('TG_API_HASH')
CHAT_ID = os.getenv('TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID')

async def main():
    client = TelegramClient('user_session', API_ID, API_HASH)
    await client.start()
    
    count = 0
    with open('debug_output.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        async for message in client.iter_messages(CHAT_ID, limit=50):
            if not message.text: continue
            
            if '💸' in message.text or 'SOL' in message.text:
                f.write("="*40 + "\n")
                f.write("TEXT:\n")
                f.write(repr(message.text) + "\n")
                f.write("ENTITIES:\n")
                if message.entities:
                    for ent in message.entities:
                        url = getattr(ent, 'url', None)
                        f.write(f"- {type(ent).__name__}: offset={ent.offset}, length={ent.length}, url={url}\n")
                        f.write(f"  extracted text: {repr(message.text[ent.offset:ent.offset+ent.length])}\n")
                else:
                    f.write("No entities\n")
                
                count += 1
                if count >= 3:
                    break

if __name__ == '__main__':
    asyncio.run(main())

FILE: d:/FruxLabs/WhaleSignalLab\feedback_loop.py

import sqlite3
import pandas as pd
import os

DB_PATH = "c:/FruxLabs/Alpha-Whales/trade_telemetry.db"
WATCHLIST_PATH = "c:/FruxLabs/Alpha-Whales/approved_watchlist.csv"

def run_feedback_loop():
    print("\nStarting Self-Healing Feedback Loop...")
    
    if not os.path.exists(DB_PATH):
        print(f"  [-] Database not found at {DB_PATH}. Skipping feedback loop.")
        return {"wallets_pruned": 0}
        
    if not os.path.exists(WATCHLIST_PATH):
        print(f"  [-] Watchlist not found at {WATCHLIST_PATH}. Skipping feedback loop.")
        return {"wallets_pruned": 0}

    # Connect to the Rust execution engine's SQLite DB
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    
    # We want to check the last 3 trades for each wallet. 
    # If all 3 are 'STOP_LOSS', they are toxic in the current market and must be pruned.
    query = """
    SELECT wallet_address, execution_status 
    FROM (
      SELECT wallet_address, execution_status,
             ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY wallet_address ORDER BY timestamp DESC) as rn
      FROM trade_logs
      WHERE trade_direction = 'SELL'
    ) 
    WHERE rn <= 3;
    """
    
    try:
        df_logs = pd.read_sql_query(query, conn)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  [-] Error querying trade_telemetry.db: {e}")
        conn.close()
        return {"wallets_pruned": 0}
        
    conn.close()

    if df_logs.empty:
        print("  [+] No trade logs found to evaluate.")
        return {"wallets_pruned": 0}

    toxic_wallets = set()
    
    # Group by wallet and evaluate
    grouped = df_logs.groupby('wallet_address')
    for wallet, group in grouped:
        # Check if they have exactly 3 trades and all 3 are 'STOP_LOSS'
        if len(group) == 3 and (group['execution_status'] == 'STOP_LOSS').all():
            toxic_wallets.add(wallet)
            
    if not toxic_wallets:
        print("  [+] All monitored whales are performing within risk parameters. No pruning needed.")
        return {"wallets_pruned": 0}
        
    print(f"  [!] Identified {len(toxic_wallets)} TOXIC whales with 3 consecutive stop-losses!")
    
    # Load watchlist
    watchlist_df = pd.read_csv(WATCHLIST_PATH)
    original_count = len(watchlist_df)
    
    # Prune toxic wallets
    if 'whale_wallet' in watchlist_df.columns:
        watchlist_df = watchlist_df[~watchlist_df['whale_wallet'].isin(toxic_wallets)]
        pruned_count = original_count - len(watchlist_df)
        
        if pruned_count > 0:
            watchlist_df.to_csv(WATCHLIST_PATH, index=False)
            for w in toxic_wallets:
                 print(f"  -> PRUNED: {w}")
            print(f"  [+] Saved cleansed watchlist. (Pruned {pruned_count} wallets).")
            print("  [+] Rust engine DashMap will hot-reload this within 60s.")
        else:
            print("  [+] Toxic wallets were already removed from the watchlist.")
            
        return {"wallets_pruned": pruned_count}
    else:
        print("  [-] 'whale_wallet' column not found in watchlist.")
        return {"wallets_pruned": 0}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_feedback_loop()

FILE: d:/FruxLabs/WhaleSignalLab\flipside_matcher.py
================================================================================

import os
import time
import pandas as pd
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from flipside import Flipside

def generate_sql(target_mint, sol_size, alert_timestamp):
    # Generates the exact Snowflake SQL query for Flipside's data studio
    # Note: Double quotes added to aliases to force lower-case JSON keys
    return f'''
    SELECT 
        swapper AS "whale_wallet",
        tx_id AS "transaction_signature",
        block_timestamp,
        swap_from_amount AS "sol_spent",
        swap_to_amount AS "tokens_bought",
        program_id AS "dex_used"
    FROM solana.defi.fact_swaps
    WHERE 
        swap_to_mint = '{target_mint}'
        AND swap_from_mint = 'So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112'
        AND swap_from_amount BETWEEN {sol_size} * 0.95 AND {sol_size} * 1.05
        AND block_timestamp BETWEEN TRY_CAST('{alert_timestamp}' AS TIMESTAMP) - INTERVAL '60 seconds'
                                AND TRY_CAST('{alert_timestamp}' AS TIMESTAMP)
    ORDER BY block_timestamp DESC
    LIMIT 1;
    '''

def main():
    # 1. Load the environment variables
    load_dotenv()
    api_key = os.getenv("FLIPSIDE_API_KEY")
    
    if not api_key:
        print("Error: FLIPSIDE_API_KEY not found in .env file.")
        print("Please grab an API key from https://flipsidecrypto.xyz/ and add it.")
        return

    # 2. Initialize the Flipside Client
    flipside = Flipside(api_key, "https://api-v2.flipsidecrypto.xyz")

    input_file = "whale_signals_raw.csv"
    output_file = "resolved_whale_wallets.csv"

    if not os.path.exists(input_file):
        print(f"Error: {input_file} not found. Run the Telethon scraper first.")
        return

    # 3. Load the scraped Telegram data
    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)
    results = []

    print(f"Loaded {len(df)} signals. Beginning Flipside on-chain resolution...")

    # 4. Loop through the rows and hit the Flipside API
    for index, row in df.iterrows():
        mint = row['token_mint']
        sol_size = row['sol_buy_size']
        timestamp = row['timestamp']

        sql = generate_sql(mint, sol_size, timestamp)
        
        print(f"[{index+1}/{len(df)}] Querying chain for {mint}...")
        
        try:
            # Execute the query
            query_result = flipside.query(sql)
            
            # Check if we got a match
            if query_result.records:
                record = query_result.records[0]
                results.append({
                    "message_id": row['message_id'],
                    "alert_timestamp": timestamp,
                    "token_mint": mint,
                    "reported_buy_size": sol_size,
                    "whale_wallet": record.get('whale_wallet'),
                    "tx_signature": record.get('transaction_signature'),
                    "actual_sol_spent": record.get('sol_spent'),
                    "dex_used": record.get('dex_used')
                })
                print(f"  -> MATCH FOUND! Wallet: {record.get('whale_wallet')}")
            else:
                print("  -> No matching transaction found on-chain (likely slippage or false alert).")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  -> API Error: {e}")
        
        # 5. Rate limiting: Flipside free tier requires some breathing room between queries
        time.sleep(2)

    # 6. Save the successfully resolved wallets to a new CSV
    if results:
        out_df = pd.DataFrame(results)
        out_df.to_csv(output_file, index=False)
        print(f"\nSuccess! Resolved {len(results)} wallets.")
        print(f"Saved cleanly to {output_file}. These are ready for the Helius rust daemon.")
    else:
        print("\nFinished process, but no wallets were matched. Check your slippage tolerance.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import os
import requests
from dotenv import load_dotenv

def sync_webhook(wallets):
    load_dotenv()
    api_key = os.getenv("HELIUS_API_KEY")
    webhook_url = os.getenv("WEBHOOK_URL") 
    
    if not api_key or not webhook_url:
        print("  [!] Helius Auto-Updater: Missing API key or Webhook URL in .env.")
        return
        
    if not wallets:
        print("  [!] Helius Auto-Updater: No wallets to monitor.")
        return
        
    base_url = f"https://api.helius.xyz/v0/webhooks"
    
    # 1. GET all webhooks
    try:
        response = requests.get(f"{base_url}?api-key={api_key}", timeout=10)
        response.raise_for_status()
        all_webhooks = response.json()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  [-] Helius Auto-Updater: Failed to fetch webhooks: {e}")
        return
        
    # Find webhooks that match our current URL
    matching_webhooks = [wh for wh in all_webhooks if wh.get('webhookURL') == webhook_url]
    
    master_webhook_id = None
    
    if len(matching_webhooks) > 0:
        master_webhook_id = matching_webhooks[0]['webhookID']
        
        # 2. DELETE duplicates
        if len(matching_webhooks) > 1:
            print(f"  [+] Helius Auto-Updater: Found {len(matching_webhooks)-1} duplicate webhooks. Deleting them...")
            for wh in matching_webhooks[1:]:
                duplicate_id = wh['webhookID']
                try:
                    requests.delete(f"{base_url}/{duplicate_id}?api-key={api_key}", timeout=10)
                    print(f"      -> Deleted duplicate: {duplicate_id}")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"      -> Failed to delete {duplicate_id}: {e}")

    payload = {
        "webhookURL": webhook_url,
        "transactionTypes": ["SWAP"],
        "accountAddresses": wallets,
        "webhookType": "enhanced"
    }

    # 3. PUT or POST
    if master_webhook_id:
        try:
            put_url = f"{base_url}/{master_webhook_id}?api-key={api_key}"
            res = requests.put(put_url, json=payload, timeout=10)
            res.raise_for_status()
            print(f"  [+] Helius Auto-Updater: Synced {len(wallets)} wallets to Webhook ({master_webhook_id[:8]}...).")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  [-] Helius Auto-Updater: Failed to update webhook: {e}")
    else:
        try:
            post_url = f"{base_url}?api-key={api_key}"
            res = requests.post(post_url, json=payload, timeout=10)
            res.raise_for_status()
            new_id = res.json().get('webhookID')
            print(f"  [+] Helius Auto-Updater: Created new Webhook ({new_id[:8]}...) with {len(wallets)} wallets.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  [-] Helius Auto-Updater: Failed to create webhook: {e}")


================================================================================
FILE: d:/FruxLabs/WhaleSignalLab\helius_matcher.py
================================================================================

import os
import time
import requests
import pandas as pd
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from dotenv import load_dotenv

def find_whale_transaction(api_key, target_mint, sol_size, alert_timestamp_str):
    dt = datetime.strptime(alert_timestamp_str, '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S').replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    base_time = int(dt.timestamp())
    
    start_time = base_time - 300
    end_time = base_time + 60
    
    url = f"https://mainnet.helius-rpc.com/?api-key={api_key}"
    
    payload = {
        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
        "id": 1,
        "method": "getTransactionsForAddress",
        "params": [
            target_mint,
            {
                "transactionDetails": "full",
                "maxSupportedTransactionVersion": 0,
                "sortOrder": "desc",
                "limit": 100,
                "filters": {
                    "blockTime": { "gte": start_time, "lte": end_time },
                    "status": "succeeded"
                }
            }
        ]
    }
    
    max_retries = 3
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            response = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=10)
            
            if response.status_code == 429:
                sleep_time = (2 ** attempt) + 1
                print(f"  -> Rate limit hit (429). Retrying in {sleep_time} seconds...")
                time.sleep(sleep_time)
                continue
                
            response.raise_for_status()
            data = response.json()
            
            if 'error' in data:
                print(f"  -> RPC Error: {data['error']}")
                return None
                
            if 'result' not in data or not isinstance(data['result'], dict) or 'data' not in data['result']:
                return None
                
            transactions = data['result']['data']
            if not transactions:
                return None
                
            target_lamports = int(sol_size * 1_000_000_000)
            tolerance = target_lamports * 0.05
            
            for tx in transactions:
                meta = tx.get('meta')
                if not meta: continue
                
                pre_balances = meta.get('preBalances', [])
                post_balances = meta.get('postBalances', [])
                if not pre_balances or not post_balances: continue
                    
                pre_bal = pre_balances[0]
                post_bal = post_balances[0]
                sol_spent = pre_bal - post_bal
                
                if abs(sol_spent - target_lamports) <= tolerance:
                    tx_info = tx.get('transaction', {})
                    msg_info = tx_info.get('message', {})
                    account_keys = msg_info.get('accountKeys', [])
                    
                    if not account_keys: continue
                        
                    signer = account_keys[0]
                    if isinstance(signer, dict): 
                        signer = signer.get('pubkey')
                        
                    signatures = tx_info.get('signatures', [])
                    tx_sig = signatures[0] if signatures else "UNKNOWN_SIGNATURE"
                        
                    return {
                        "whale_wallet": signer,
                        "tx_signature": tx_sig,
                        "actual_sol_spent": sol_spent / 1_000_000_000,
                        "block_time": tx.get('blockTime')
                    }
            return None
        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
            print(f"  -> Network/API Error on attempt {attempt+1}: {e}")
            time.sleep((2 ** attempt) + 1)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"  -> Unexpected Error: {e}")
            return None
            
    return None

def main():
    load_dotenv()
    api_key = os.getenv("HELIUS_API_KEY")
    if not api_key: 
        print("Error: HELIUS_API_KEY not found in .env")
        return
    
    input_file = "whale_signals_raw.csv"
    output_file = "resolved_whale_wallets.csv"
    if not os.path.exists(input_file): 
        print(f"Error: Input file {input_file} not found.")
        return

    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)
    results = []
    total_rows = len(df)

    print(f"Initiating matching pipeline for {total_rows} signals...\n")

    for index, row in df.iterrows():
        mint = row['token_mint']
        sol_size = row['sol_buy_size']
        timestamp = row['timestamp']
        
        match = find_whale_transaction(api_key, mint, sol_size, timestamp)
        if match:
            match['message_id'] = row['message_id']
            match['alert_timestamp'] = timestamp
            match['token_mint'] = mint
            match['reported_buy_size'] = sol_size
            results.append(match)
            print(f"[{index+1}/{total_rows}] [+] MATCH: {mint[:8]}... -> {match['whale_wallet']}")
        else:
            print(f"[{index+1}/{total_rows}] [-] MISS: No transaction found for {mint[:8]}...")
            
        time.sleep(0.5)

    if results:
        out_df = pd.DataFrame(results)[['message_id', 'alert_timestamp', 'token_mint', 'reported_buy_size', 'whale_wallet', 'tx_signature', 'actual_sol_spent']]
        out_df.to_csv(output_file, index=False)
        print(f"\nSUCCESS: Extracted {len(results)} whale wallets to {output_file}")
    else:
        print("\nFAILURE: No wallets were matched.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: d:/FruxLabs/WhaleSignalLab\leaderboard_scraper.py
================================================================================

import os
import time
import json
import subprocess
import pandas as pd
from collections import defaultdict
from dotenv import load_dotenv

# Load environment variables
load_dotenv()
GMGN_API_KEY = os.getenv("GMGN_API_KEY")

# Configuration and Thresholds
MIN_ROI_PERCENT = 1000.0  # 10x return
MIN_COIN_APPEARANCES = 3
MIN_INVESTED_SOL = 5.0
TOP_COINS_LIMIT = 20

def run_gmgn_cli(command_args):
    """Executes the gmgn-cli via npx and returns the parsed JSON."""
    try:
        # We use npx to ensure we hit the installed gmgn-cli package
        cmd = ["npx.cmd", "gmgn-cli"] + command_args
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, encoding='utf-8')
        return json.loads(result.stdout)
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        print(f"CLI Error running {' '.join(cmd)}: {e.stderr}")
        return None
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        print(f"JSON Parse Error: {e}")
        return None

def fetch_trending_coins():
    print(f"Fetching top trending coins from GMGN CLI...")
    # gmgn-cli market trending --chain sol --interval 24h
    data = run_gmgn_cli(["market", "trending", "--chain", "sol", "--interval", "24h"])
    if not data or "data" not in data:
        return []
        
    coins = data["data"].get("rank", [])
    
    addresses = []
    for coin in coins:
        addr = coin.get("address")
        if addr:
            addresses.append(addr)
    return addresses[:TOP_COINS_LIMIT]

def fetch_top_traders(coin_address):
    print(f"Fetching top traders for {coin_address}...")
    # gmgn-cli token traders --chain sol --address <address> --order-by profit --direction desc
    data = run_gmgn_cli(["token", "traders", "--chain", "sol", "--address", coin_address, "--order-by", "profit", "--direction", "desc"])
    
    if not data or "data" not in data:
        return []
    
    # The API returns a list of traders in 'data' directly sometimes, or wrapped.
    return data["data"]

def run_scraper():
    print("Starting Leaderboard Scraper (via GMGN CLI)...")
    coins = fetch_trending_coins()
    if not coins:
        print("No trending coins found. Please verify the GMGN API endpoint or your API Key.")
        return {"candidates_found": 0}

    # Dictionary to track wallet stats across coins
    # wallet_address -> { "coins_traded": set(), "total_profit": 0, "total_invested": 0, "rois": [] }
    wallets = defaultdict(lambda: {"coins_traded": set(), "total_profit": 0.0, "total_invested": 0.0, "rois": []})

    for coin in coins:
        traders = fetch_top_traders(coin)
        for t in traders:
            # GMGN top trader endpoints use 'address' or 'maker'
            address = t.get("address") or t.get("maker")
            if not address:
                continue
            
            # The CLI usually returns realized_pnl as decimal multiplier (e.g., 24.84 for 2484%)
            # Let's extract ROI or PNL. 
            # In the new CLI format, it might be `realized_pnl` or `profit_change`
            pnl_decimal = float(t.get("realized_pnl") or t.get("profit_change") or t.get("roi") or 0)
            
            profit = float(t.get("realized_profit") or t.get("profit") or 0)
            invested = float(t.get("total_cost") or t.get("history_bought_cost") or 0)
            
            normalized_roi = pnl_decimal * 100 if pnl_decimal < 1000 else pnl_decimal
            
            if normalized_roi >= MIN_ROI_PERCENT:
                wallets[address]["coins_traded"].add(coin)
                wallets[address]["total_profit"] += profit
                wallets[address]["total_invested"] += invested
                wallets[address]["rois"].append(normalized_roi)
        
        # Short sleep to prevent aggressively spamming the CLI/API
        time.sleep(1.5)

    # Filter whales based on aggregate metrics
    candidates = []
    for address, stats in wallets.items():
        if len(stats["coins_traded"]) >= MIN_COIN_APPEARANCES and stats["total_invested"] >= MIN_INVESTED_SOL:
            candidates.append({
                "wallet_address": address,
                "coins_sniped": len(stats["coins_traded"]),
                "total_invested_sol": round(stats["total_invested"], 2),
                "total_profit_sol": round(stats["total_profit"], 2),
                "avg_roi_percent": round(sum(stats["rois"]) / len(stats["rois"]), 2)
            })

    df = pd.DataFrame(candidates)
    if not df.empty:
        df = df.sort_values("coins_sniped", ascending=False)
        df.to_csv("candidate_whales.csv", index=False)
        print(f"Successfully exported {len(df)} whale candidates to candidate_whales.csv")
        return {"candidates_found": len(df)}
    else:
        print("No wallets met the strict edge criteria (>= 1000% ROI on >= 3 coins with >= 5 SOL).")
        return {"candidates_found": 0}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_scraper()

import os
import requests
import pandas as pd
from dotenv import load_dotenv

def main():
    load_dotenv()
    api_key = os.getenv("HELIUS_API_KEY")
    webhook_url = os.getenv("WEBHOOK_URL") 
    
    if not api_key:
        print("Error: HELIUS_API_KEY not found in .env")
        return
        
    if not webhook_url:
        print("Error: WEBHOOK_URL not found in .env.")
        print("Please add WEBHOOK_URL to your .env file (e.g. your ngrok url like https://your-ngrok.app/webhook)")
        return
        
    # For our live trading signals, let's track the highly profitable whales
    input_file = "approved_watchlist.csv"
    if not os.path.exists(input_file):
        print(f"Error: {input_file} not found. Run wallet_scorer.py first.")
        return
        
    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)
    wallets = df['whale_wallet'].dropna().unique().tolist()
    
    if not wallets:
        print("No wallets found in the watchlist.")
        return
        
    print(f"Registering {len(wallets)} Alpha wallets to Helius Webhook: {webhook_url}")
    
    payload = {
        "webhookURL": webhook_url,
        "transactionTypes": ["SWAP"],
        "accountAddresses": wallets,
        "webhookType": "enhanced"
    }
    
    url = f"https://api.helius.xyz/v0/webhooks?api-key={api_key}"
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=10)
    
    if response.status_code in [200, 201]:
        print("\n[SUCCESS]: Webhook registered and active!")
        print(f"Webhook ID: {response.json().get('webhookID')}")
        print("Helius is now monitoring these wallets. Start webhook_server.py to receive signals.")
    else:
        print("\n[ERROR] registering webhook:")
        print(response.text)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: d:/FruxLabs/WhaleSignalLab\scraper.py
================================================================================

import os
import re
import asyncio
import pandas as pd
from telethon import TelegramClient
from telethon.tl.types import MessageEntityTextUrl, MessageEntityUrl
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from datetime import timezone

load_dotenv()
API_ID = int(os.getenv('TG_API_ID'))
API_HASH = os.getenv('TG_API_HASH')
CHAT_ID = int(os.getenv('TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID'))

SOL_MINT_REGEX = r"\b([1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44})\b"
SOL_BUY_REGEX = r"💸\s*([0-9.]+)\s*SOL\s*→"
TICKER_REGEX = r"\$([A-Za-z0-9_]+)"

def extract_mint_from_urls(urls):
    for url in urls:
        # Ignore social links that aren't token mints
        if any(ignored in url.lower() for ignored in ['t.me', 'twitter.com', 'x.com']):
            continue
        match = re.search(SOL_MINT_REGEX, url)
        if match:
            return match.group(1)
    return None

async def main():
    client = TelegramClient('user_session', API_ID, API_HASH)
    await client.start()
    
    print(f"Connected to Telegram User Account. Fetching history from {CHAT_ID}...")
    
    extracted_data = []
    
    async for message in client.iter_messages(CHAT_ID, limit=1000):
        if not message.text:
            continue
            
        text = message.text
        
        # Clean markdown asterisks to make regex matching easier
        clean_text = text.replace('*', '')
        
        # Filter for actual buy/accumulation alerts
        sol_match = re.search(SOL_BUY_REGEX, clean_text)
        if not sol_match:
            continue
            
        sol_size = float(sol_match.group(1))
        
        # Extract embedded URLs from Telethon entities
        urls = []
        if message.entities:
            for entity in message.entities:
                if isinstance(entity, MessageEntityTextUrl):
                    urls.append(entity.url)
                elif isinstance(entity, MessageEntityUrl):
                    # Extract literal URL string from message text
                    url_str = text[entity.offset:entity.offset + entity.length]
                    urls.append(url_str)
                    
        mint = extract_mint_from_urls(urls)
        
        # Fallback: Check plain text for mint if not found in entities
        if not mint:
            plain_mint_match = re.search(SOL_MINT_REGEX, text)
            if plain_mint_match:
                mint = plain_mint_match.group(1)
                
        ticker_match = re.search(TICKER_REGEX, text)
        ticker = ticker_match.group(1) if ticker_match else "UNKNOWN"
        
        if mint and sol_size:
            timestamp = message.date.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            extracted_data.append({
                "message_id": message.id,
                "timestamp": timestamp.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),
                "ticker": ticker,
                "token_mint": mint,
                "sol_buy_size": sol_size
            })

    if extracted_data:
        df = pd.DataFrame(extracted_data)
        df.to_csv("whale_signals_raw.csv", index=False)
        print(f"SUCCESS: Extracted {len(extracted_data)} signals into whale_signals_raw.csv")
    else:
        print("WARNING: Processed messages but extracted 0 signals. Check parsing logic.")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    asyncio.run(main())

import requests

def test_dexscreener(mint):
    url = f"https://api.dexscreener.com/latest/dex/tokens/{mint}"
    resp = requests.get(url).json()
    pairs = resp.get("pairs", [])
    if pairs:
        print("Price Native (SOL):", pairs[0].get("priceNative"))
        print("Price USD:", pairs[0].get("priceUsd"))
    else:
        print("No pairs found.")

FILE: d:/FruxLabs/WhaleSignalLab\wallet_scorer.py

import os
import time
import json
import subprocess
import db_manager
import helius_manager

def run_gmgn_stats(wallet):
    """Executes the gmgn-cli to fetch wallet portfolio stats."""
    cmd = [
        "npx.cmd", "gmgn-cli", "portfolio", "stats", 
        "--chain", "sol", 
        "--wallet", wallet,
        "--period", "30d"
    ]
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, encoding='utf-8', timeout=15)
        return json.loads(result.stdout)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  -> CLI Error fetching stats for {wallet[:8]}: {e}", flush=True)
        return None

def main():
    print("Initiating Continuous Scoring Engine (Level 3)...\n")
    
    # Check if DB exists
    if not os.path.exists(db_manager.DB_PATH):
        print("Waiting for candidates.db to be initialized by scrapers...")
        return {"wallets_analyzed": 0, "wallets_approved": 0, "wallets_rejected": 0}
        
    # Get a small batch of wallets that haven't been scored recently
    batch_size = 25
    wallets_to_score = db_manager.get_wallets_to_score(limit=batch_size)
    
    if wallets_to_score.empty:
        print("No wallets need scoring at this time. All caught up!")
        return {"wallets_analyzed": 0, "wallets_approved": 0, "wallets_rejected": 0}
        
    print(f"Pulled {len(wallets_to_score)} wallets from candidates.db for scoring...")
    
    analyzed_count = 0
    approved_count = 0
    rejected_count = 0
    
    for i, row in wallets_to_score.iterrows():
        wallet = row['wallet_address']
        is_sniper = bool(row['is_sniper'])
        
        stats = run_gmgn_stats(wallet)
        if not stats:
            time.sleep(1)
            continue
            
        analyzed_count += 1
        
        realized_profit = float(stats.get("realized_profit", 0))
        total_buy = int(stats.get("buy", 0))
        total_sell = int(stats.get("sell", 0))
        total_trades = total_buy + total_sell
        
        pnl_stat = stats.get("pnl_stat", {})
        win_rate = float(pnl_stat.get("winrate", 0))
        avg_hold_time_secs = float(pnl_stat.get("avg_holding_period", 0))
        avg_hold_hours = avg_hold_time_secs / 3600
        
        if avg_hold_hours < 1.0:
            hold_display = f"{avg_hold_time_secs / 60:.1f}m"
        else:
            hold_display = f"{avg_hold_hours:.1f}h"
            
        print(f"[{i+1}/{len(wallets_to_score)}] {wallet[:8]}... | Net: {realized_profit:.2f} SOL | WR: {win_rate:.0%} | Avg Hold: {hold_display}")

        # Determine Lane
        lane = "Rejected"
        
        if is_sniper and realized_profit > 10.0 and win_rate >= 0.40:
            lane = "Sniper"
        elif win_rate >= 0.60 and realized_profit >= 10.0 and avg_hold_hours > 24:
            lane = "Conservative"
        elif win_rate >= 0.40 and realized_profit >= 50.0 and avg_hold_hours > 6:
            lane = "Swing"
        elif win_rate >= 0.20 and realized_profit >= 100.0:
            lane = "Degen"
            
        if lane != "Rejected":
            approved_count += 1
        else:
            rejected_count += 1
            
        # Insert time-series score
        db_manager.insert_score(wallet, win_rate, realized_profit, avg_hold_hours, lane)
        time.sleep(1.5) # Prevent GMGN CLI API bans
        
    print("\n--- RESULTS ---")
    print(f"Analyzed: {analyzed_count} | Passed: {approved_count} | Rejected: {rejected_count}")
    
    # Generate the active watchlist
    approved_wallets = db_manager.generate_approved_watchlist()
    total_approved = len(approved_wallets)
    print(f"\nSUCCESS: Generated active approved_watchlist.csv with {total_approved} elite whales!")
    print("The Rust Engine will automatically reload this new watchlist without restarting!")
    
    # Sync with Helius
    helius_manager.sync_webhook(approved_wallets)
    
    return {
        "wallets_analyzed": analyzed_count,
        "wallets_approved": approved_count,
        "wallets_rejected": rejected_count
    }

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
import uvicorn
import json

app = FastAPI()

@app.post("/webhook")
async def receive_webhook(request: Request):
    try:
        data = await request.json()
        print("\n" + "="*50)
        print("🚨 WHALE TRADE DETECTED 🚨")
        print("="*50)
        
        # Helius sends an array of transaction objects
        for tx in data:
            description = tx.get('description', '')
            if description:
                print(f"Signal: {description}")
            else:
                print(f"Raw Tx Hash: {tx.get('signature')}")
                
            # Log the full payload to a file for debugging/analysis
            with open("webhook_payloads.jsonl", "a") as f:
                f.write(json.dumps(tx) + "\n")
                
        return {"status": "ok"}
    except Exception as e:
        print("Error processing webhook:", e)
        return {"status": "error"}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("Starting Whale Signal Listener on port 5000...")
    print("Waiting for Helius to push trades...")
    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=5000)
